<script>
    $(document).ready(function () {
        hideNewsMenuItem();
    });

    var backdrop = $('#mega-menu-backdrop');
    var body = $(document.body);
    var fromTop = window.pageYOffset;
    var visible;

    $(document).scroll(function () {
        if (!visible) {
            fromTop = window.pageYOffset;
        }

    });

    $('.header__navigation #mega-menu-1').mouseover(function () {
        visible = true;
        body.css('top', -fromTop);
        body.toggleClass('noscroll', true);
        backdrop.toggleClass('visible', true);
    });

    $('.header__navigation #mega-menu-1').mouseleave(function () {
        visible = false;
        body.css('top', 0);
        body.toggleClass('noscroll', false);

        $(window).scrollTop(fromTop);

        backdrop.toggleClass('visible', false);
    });

    function hideNewsMenuItem() {
        var newsMenuitem = $('.header__navigation #mega-menu-3');
        var lang = "et-EE";
        var pattern = /en-US/i;
        if (pattern.test(lang)) {
            newsMenuitem.toggleClass('hidden', true);
        } else {
            newsMenuitem.toggleClass('hidden', false);
        }
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
                    $(document).ready(function () {
                        $(".js-header-shopping-cart .cart__total").text($(".flyout-cart .totals strong").text());
                        $(".js-header-shopping-cart").clone(true, true).appendTo(".js-header-shopping-cart-clone");

                        var timeout;

                        $('.header, .fixed-header').on('mouseenter', '.js-cartlink', function () {
                            var $cartLink = $(this);
                            var $container = $cartLink.closest(".js-header-shopping-cart");
                            var $flyoutCart = $(".flyout-cart", $container);
                            clearTimeout(timeout);
                            $flyoutCart.addClass('active');
                        });

                        $('.header, .fixed-header').on('mouseleave', '.js-cartlink', function () {
                            var $cartLink = $(this);
                            var $container = $cartLink.closest(".js-header-shopping-cart");
                            var $flyoutCart = $(".flyout-cart", $container);

                            timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                                $flyoutCart.removeClass('active');
                            }, 100);
                        });

                        $('.header, .fixed-header').on('mouseenter', '.flyout-cart', function () {
                            var $flyoutCart = $(this);

                            clearTimeout(timeout);
                            $flyoutCart.addClass('active');
                        });

                        $('.header, .fixed-header').on('mouseleave', '.flyout-cart', function () {
                            var $flyoutCart = $(this);

                            timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                                $flyoutCart.removeClass('active');
                            }, 100);
                        });

                        $(document).on("nopAjaxCartProductAddedToCartEvent", function (e) {
                            $(".js-header-shopping-cart").each(function () {
                                var $cartContainer = $(this);

                                var $cartQty = $(".cart__link", $cartContainer);
                                var oldVal = parseInt($cartQty.data("value"));
                                var newVal = oldVal + parseInt(e.quantity);
                                $cartQty.attr("data-value", newVal).data("value", newVal);
                            });

                            console.log(e);
                        });
                    });
                </script>

<script>
    var fixedHeader = $('.fixed-header');

    function debounce(func, wait, immediate) {
        var timeout;

        return function () {
            var context = this, args = arguments;
            var later = function () {
                timeout = null;
                if (!immediate) func.apply(context, args);
            };
            var callNow = immediate && !timeout;
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
            if (callNow) func.apply(context, args);
        };
    }

    var handleScroll = debounce(function () {
        if (window.innerWidth > 980) {
            var topPosition = $('.master-wrapper-page').offset().top;

            if (window.scrollY > topPosition) {
                fixedHeader.toggleClass('visible', true);
            } else {
                fixedHeader.toggleClass('visible', false);
            }
        }
    }, 250);

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        function newsletter_subscribe(subscribe) {
            var subscribeProgress = $("#subscribe-loading-progress");
            subscribeProgress.show();
            var postData = {
                subscribe: subscribe,
                email: $("#newsletter-email").val()
            };
            $.ajax({
                cache: false,
                type: "POST",
                url: "/ee/subscribenewsletter",
                data: postData,
                success: function(data) {
                    subscribeProgress.hide();
                    $("#newsletter-result-block").html(data.Result);
                    if (data.Success) {
                        $('#newsletter-subscribe-block').hide();
                        $('#newsletter-result-block').show();
                    } else {
                        $('#newsletter-result-block').fadeIn("slow").delay(2000).fadeOut("slow");
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
                    alert('Failed to subscribe.');
                    subscribeProgress.hide();
                }
            });
        }

        $(document).ready(function () {
            $('#newsletter-subscribe-button').click(function () {
newsletter_subscribe('true');            });
            $("#newsletter-email").keydown(function (event) {
                if (event.keyCode == 13) {
                    $("#newsletter-subscribe-button").click();
                    return false;
                }
            });
        });
    </script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        $('#eu-cookie-bar-notification').show();

        $('#eu-cookie-ok').click(function () {
            $.ajax({
                cache: false,
                type: 'POST',
                url: '/eucookielawaccept',
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function (data) {
                    $('#eu-cookie-bar-notification').hide();
                },
                failure: function () {
                    alert('Cannot store value');
                }
            });
        });
    });
</script>
